(The recorded entry identifies the coefficient vector by a program without recording its defining formula. The paper specifies the minimum-Euclidean-norm vector explicitly; the subsequent computation on a retained spectral span does not replace the unrestricted vector in Proposition~\ref{prop:rkhs_qp}.)
- note on `symbol \xi_n`: symbol table corrected: space "$\{(\xi_n)_{n\geqslant1}:\xi_n>0,\ \xi_n\downarrow0\}$" (The deterministic sequence decreasing to zero is correctly described, but the recorded space requires every term to be strictly below one. The paper explicitly allows $\xi_n=1/\log(en)$, whose first term equals one; the tuning rules use confidence levels $\xi_n/2$.)
- note on `symbol a_j`: symbol table corrected: space "$[0,\infty)$, with $a_0=0$ and $a_j\geqslant1$ for $j=1,\ldots,t$" (The recorded space excludes the explicitly used initialization $a_0=0$. The paper fixes the positive-index constants uniformly over sample size and penalty. Proposition~\ref{prop:rkhs_qp} uses coefficient symbols $\alpha_j$, not the RDIV constants $a_j$, so it introduces no second meaning.)
- note on `symbol A_n`: symbol table corrected: def "$A_n(\lambda)$ is the noise level supplied to the discrepancy rule, whose acceptance threshold is $\tfrac32A_n(\lambda)$." (The recorded prescribed-bound definition excludes the experimental noise-floor meaning. The existing general reading and theoretical formulas remain valid; four clauses require retagging.)
- note on `symbol L`: symbol table corrected: type "method-specific population loss", def "RDIV uses $L(h)=\mathbb E[(Y-\mathcal T h(Z))^2]=\sigma^2_{Y\mid Z}+\\|\mathcal T(h-h_0)\\|^2$, and TRAE uses $L(h)=\sup_{f\in\mathcal F}\mathbb E[2m(W;f)-2h(X)f(Z)-f(Z)^2]$." (The table gives only the RDIV population squared loss. The existing method-specific correction remains valid; the additional clause specifies the adversarial meaning in the separately registered iterative TRAE concentration steps.)
- note on `symbol L_n`: symbol table corrected: def "For RDIV, $L_n(h)=\mathbb E_{n,\mathrm{fit}}[(Y-\hat{\mathcal T}h(Z))^2]$. For TRAE, $L_n(h)=\sup_{f\in\mathcal F}\mathbb E_n[2m(W;f)-2h(X)f(Z)-f(Z)^2]$, where $\mathbb E_n$ averages over its fitting sample." (The table records only the RDIV squared loss, whereas TRAE reuses the symbol for an adversarial supremum. The existing fix remains valid; the additional scopes supply these meanings to the separately registered iterative concentration steps.)
- note on `symbol P_\eta`: symbol table corrected: type "ridge-stabilized critic smoothing matrix" (The displayed formula is correct, but the matrix is generally not an idempotent projection: its eigenvalues are $d/(d+N\eta)$ for eigenvalues $d$ of $K_{\mathcal F}$. Proposition~\ref{prop:rkhs_qp} refers back to the same definition.)
- note on `symbol q`: symbol table corrected: type "test function or scalar bias exponent, according to local context", space "$\tilde{\mathcal Q}\cup(1,\infty)$", def "The letter $q$ denotes a function in the moment and dual-estimation formulas, and is separately reused for the scalar exponent $q>1$ in $B_n(\lambda)=C_b\lambda^q$." (The table records only a test function, but the generic discrepancy theorem and four rate proofs also use $q$ as a scalar exponent. The paper explicitly distinguishes these local uses, and the dual optimization restricts its function-valued candidate to $\mathcal Q$.)
- note on `thm:common-dp`: pinned reading: On $\mathcal E_n$, the algorithm terminates through its acceptance branch at an index $k\leqslant k^\circ$, where $\lambda^\circ=2\rho^{k^\circ}$ and $k^\circ\leqslant K_n$; acceptance at $k=K_n$ is allowed, and both error bounds hold on this event. (The prose following the algorithm restricts acceptance guarantees to the stated events. The proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:common_dp} establishes acceptance at the oracle point and explicitly identifies the consequence as avoiding fallback.)
- note on `thm:deepiv-rate`: pinned reading: For each fixed $n\geqslant N_{\mathrm R}(B)$, the displayed error bounds and, when $h_0=0$, both sharper $O(\delta_n^2)$ bounds hold jointly with probability at least $1-\xi$ over the preliminary, fitting, and calibration samples. (The proof obtains the zero-signal bounds from the probabilistic initial-acceptance conclusion of Proposition~\ref{prop:exist_lambda_deepiv} and Lemma~\ref{lemma:str_dp}; the general case uses the common RDIV event and the stated confidence convention.)
- note on `thm:iter-trae-rate`: pinned reading: The constants hidden by $O_t(\cdot)$ may depend on the prescribed $t$, fixed population quantities including $\beta$ and $\\|w_0\\|$, and the fixed grid ratio $\rho$, but are independent of $n$ and the candidate $\lambda$. (The text following the bounds in Theorem~\ref{thm:iter_rdiv_rate} explicitly allows dependence on the prescribed iteration count and fixed population quantities; Lemma~\ref{lemma:iter_trae_accepted_stability} supplies the comparison constants used here under the same convention.)
- note on `thm:rdiv-discrepancy-bound`: pinned reading: The assumptions imported from Theorem~\ref{thm:deepiv_rate} are the general hypotheses of Proposition~\ref{prop:exist_lambda_deepiv}, without imposing $h_0=0$. (Theorem~\ref{thm:deepiv_rate} imports the proposition's assumptions and treats $h_0=0$ only in a separate conditional conclusion. The present proof uses Lemmas~\ref{lemma:err_l_dp} and~\ref{lemma:str_dp} for general $h_0$.)
- note on `thm:tikhonv-rate`: pinned reading: With $\beta$ and $\xi$ fixed as $n\to\infty$, for each $n\geqslant N_{\mathrm T}(B)$ the displayed strong- and weak-error bounds hold jointly on a fitting-sample event of probability at least $1-\xi$. (The proof of Theorem~\ref{thm:tikhonv_rate} works on $\mathcal E_n$, which the preceding confidence allocation constructs separately for each sample size.)
